# Agents · J · part 2

972 entries, jev-critical to joyus-dev. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **jev-critical**: Sensitive or high-impact delegated work requiring careful review and verification. Use when Jev recommends CRITICAL and the parent delegates. ([shaul1991/jev_route](https://github.com/shaul1991/jev_route/blob/HEAD/cursor-plugin/agents/jev-critical.md))
- **jev-debugger**: Coder of last resort (high effort): only when builder and engineer attempts got stuck on the same problem. Never the first choice. ([dansya-arsana/jev-harness](https://github.com/dansya-arsana/jev-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/jev-debugger.md) · ★2)
- **jev-decision-engineer**: Use when adding bounded Jev classification, routing, confidence policy, or failure behavior to Scaffolder. Jev recommends; deterministic generators and tests remain authoritative. ([judigot/scaffolder](https://github.com/judigot/scaffolder/blob/HEAD/agents/jev-decision-engineer.md))
- **jev-deep**: Repository worker pinned to the deep tier (opus). Use when you already know the tier you want and would rather skip the routing call — hard reasoning, real ambiguity, or a change that is expensive to get wrong. For anything else prefer jev-worker, which is priced per brief. ([leftspace89/jevsubrouter](https://github.com/leftspace89/jevsubrouter/blob/HEAD/agents/jev-deep.md) · ★4)
- **jev-deep**: Top-tier model for complex, subtle, or high-stakes work escalated by jev routing — hard debugging, architecture and design decisions, security-sensitive or data-migration changes. - **jev-engineer**: Coder for substantial coordinated multi-file changes (medium effort). Used only when the work is bigger than a builder task, or when a builder got stuck. ([dansya-arsana/jev-harness](https://github.com/dansya-arsana/jev-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/jev-engineer.md) · ★2)

- **jev-locator**: Read-only code locator for jev-flow. Give it one question about where behavior lives in the current repository; it runs the local candidate helper, which applies the ranking rule itself (jev_rerank or jev_find with top_k 5 when candidates are ambiguous) and prints only compact ranked hits, verifies the top hits by reading them, and returns at most 5 compact locations with sha256 and line ranges. - **jev-review**: Rerun on 2026-11-10 at executable head `b60b49c`. The earlier subprocess did not inherit `TYPESAFE_API_KEY`; the credential already existed in the interactive shell and macOS app launch environment. Running the installed tools through `/bin/zsh -lic` restored access without printing, persisting or changing the key. This supersedes the earlier claim that a credential needed to be installed. ([JoshuaOliphant/forkhub](https://github.com/JoshuaOliphant/forkhub/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/jev-review.md) · ★1)

The post-run sweep closes a provider's jobs unseen for 48h — if a posting drops off a board we crawl, it closes. A reappearing posting reopens via the upsert (the `ON C ([MASTER-UTKARSH/AI-Resume-Automation](https://github.com/MASTER-UTKARSH/AI-Resume-Automation/blob/HEAD/reference_repos/freehire/docs/agents/job-lifecycle.md))
